\u2502  \u2022 `youtube.upload` publica v\u00eddeos \u2014 mas N\u00c3O L\u00ca v\u00eddeos nem lista o canal.       \u2502\n> \u2502  \u2022 A grade de v\u00eddeos do painel exige  `youtube.readonly`  (videos.list /        \u2502\n> \u2502    search.forMine / playlistItems.list sobre a playlist de uploads).            \u2502\n> \u2502  \u2022 TODA m\u00e9trica (views, watch-time, reten\u00e7\u00e3o, tr\u00e1fego) exige                    \u2502\n> \u2502    `yt-analytics.readonly`;  receita exige `yt-analytics-monetary.readonly`.    \u2502\n> \u2502  \u2022 Editar metadados/thumbnail/playlist exige `youtube` (ou `youtube.force-ssl`).\u2502\n> \u2502  \u2022 Moderar/deletar coment\u00e1rios e captions exige `youtube.force-ssl`.            \u2502\n> \u2502                                                                                 \u2502\n> \u2502  \u21d2 Fatia 1 (leitura+m\u00e9tricas) est\u00e1 BLOQUEADA at\u00e9 o re-consent OAuth incremental \u2502\n> \u2502    incluir, no m\u00ednimo:  youtube.readonly + yt-analytics.readonly.               \u2502\n> \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n> ```\n> **Escopos m\u00ednimos por Fatia:** Fatia 1 (read+metrics) = `youtube.readonly` + `yt-analytics.readonly` (+ `-monetary.readonly` se surface de receita). Fatia 2 (write seguro) = `youtube` (metadados/playlist) + `youtube.upload` (thumbnail) + `youtube.force-ssl` (coment\u00e1rio). Fatia 3 (destrutivo) = `youtube.force-ssl` (delete de v\u00eddeo/coment\u00e1rio/caption) + `youtube` (delete de playlist/se\u00e7\u00e3o/subscription).\n\n### \u00a71.1 Fluxo OAuth (installed / desktop \u2014 verificado em `installed-apps`)\n\nPKCE obrigat\u00f3rio (`code_verifier` 43\u2013128 chars \u2192 `code_challenge` S256). Loopback IP (`http://127.0.0.1:port`) \u2014 custom URI schemes descontinuados. Token exchange em `POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token` retorna `access_token`+`refresh_token`+`scope`; **inspecionar `scope` concedido** (usu\u00e1rio pode n\u00e3o conceder todos \u2014 \u00e9 exatamente o que produziu o gap acima). Revoga\u00e7\u00e3o: `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/revoke?token=`. Fonte: [`scopes-quota.md \u00a71.1`](./_apimap/scopes-quota.md).\n\n---\n\n## \u00a72 Recursos da Data API v3 \u2014 propriedades por recurso\n\n> **Estas tabelas s\u00e3o as COLUNAS que a tabela CRUD do painel renderiza.** `W` = writable (mut\u00e1vel via insert/update); demais = read-only. Fontes citadas por recurso.\n\n### 2.1 `videos` \u2014 parts: `snippet \u00b7 contentDetails \u00b7 status \u00b7 statistics \u00b7 player \u00b7 topicDetails \u00b7 recordingDetails \u00b7 fileDetails \u00b7 processingDetails \u00b7 suggestions \u00b7 liveStreamingDetails \u00b7 paidProductPlacementDetails \u00b7 localizations` (fonte: [`videos.md`](./_apimap/videos.md))\n\n| Part | Propriedade | Tipo | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|---|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet | publishedAt | datetime | | Timestamp de publica\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| snippet | channelId | string | | ID do canal |\n| snippet | title | string(\u2264100) | \u2705 | T\u00edtulo (sem `<` `>`) |\n| snippet | description | string(\u22645000 B) | \u2705 | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| snippet | thumbnails | object | | Mapa default/medium/high/standard/maxres |\n| snippet | channelTitle | string | | Nome do canal |\n| snippet | tags[] | list<string> | \u2705 | Tags (\u2264500 chars total) |\n| snippet | categoryId | string | \u2705 | Categoria (obrigat\u00f3rio se enviar snippet no update) |\n| snippet | liveBroadcastContent | string | | `live`/`none`/`upcoming` |\n| snippet | defaultLanguage | string | \u2705 | Idioma dos metadados |\n| snippet | localized | object | | T\u00edtulo/descri\u00e7\u00e3o resolvidos por `hl` (read) |\n| snippet | defaultAudioLanguage | string | \u2705 | Idioma de \u00e1udio prim\u00e1rio |\n| contentDetails | duration | ISO8601 | | Dura\u00e7\u00e3o (`PT15M33S`) |\n| contentDetails | dimension | string | | `2d`/`3d` |\n| contentDetails | definition | string | | `hd`/`sd` |\n| contentDetails | caption | boolean | | Legenda dispon\u00edvel |\n| contentDetails | licensedContent | boolean | | Conte\u00fado licenciado/claimed |\n| contentDetails | regionRestriction | object | \u2705 | `allowed[]`/`blocked[]` geogr\u00e1fico |\n| contentDetails | contentRating | object | \u2705 | 67 campos de rating por jurisdi\u00e7\u00e3o (`djctqRating`=BR, `ytRating`=age-restricted) |\n| contentDetails | projection | string | | `360`/`rectangular` |\n| contentDetails | hasCustomThumbnail | boolean | | Thumbnail custom |\n| status | uploadStatus | string | | `deleted`/`failed`/`processed`/`rejected`/`uploaded` |\n| status | failureReason | string | | Motivo de falha de upload |\n| status | rejectionReason | string | | Motivo de rejei\u00e7\u00e3o YouTube |\n| status | privacyStatus | string | \u2705 | `private`/`public`/`unlisted` |\n| status | publishAt | datetime | \u2705 | Publica\u00e7\u00e3o agendada (s\u00f3 p/ `private`) |\n| status | license | string | \u2705 | `creativeCommon`/`youtube` |\n| status | embeddable | boolean | \u2705 | Permite embed |\n| status | publicStatsViewable | boolean | \u2705 | Estat\u00edsticas vis\u00edveis |\n| status | madeForKids | boolean | | Direcionado a crian\u00e7as |\n| status | selfDeclaredMadeForKids | boolean | \u2705 | Autodeclara\u00e7\u00e3o do dono |\n| status | containsSyntheticMedia | boolean | \u2705 | Flag de conte\u00fado alterado/sint\u00e9tico (IA) |\n| statistics | viewCount | ulong | | Views |\n| statistics | likeCount | ulong | | Likes |\n| statistics | dislikeCount | ulong | | Dislikes (owner-only desde 2021-12) |\n| statistics | favoriteCount | ulong | | Deprecated, sempre 0 |\n| statistics | commentCount | ulong | | Coment\u00e1rios |\n| player | embedHtml | string | | `<iframe>` de playback |\n| player | embedHeight/embedWidth | long | | Dimens\u00f5es (se `maxHeight`/`maxWidth` no list) |\n| topicDetails | relevantTopicIds[]/topicCategories[] | list | \u2705 | T\u00f3picos (deprecated) / URIs Wikipedia |\n| recordingDetails | recordingDate | datetime | \u2705 | Data de grava\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| recordingDetails | location | object | \u2705 | Geolocaliza\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| paidProductPlacementDetails | hasPaidProductPlacement | boolean | \u2705 | Flag sponsored |\n| localizations | (key).title/.description | object | \u2705 | Metadados traduzidos (BCP-47) |\n| fileDetails (owner) | fileName/fileSize/fileType/container/videoStreams[]/audioStreams[]/durationMs/bitrateBps/creationTime | v\u00e1rios | | 9 props read-only do arquivo-fonte |\n| processingDetails (owner) | processingStatus/processingProgress/\u2026 (8 props) | v\u00e1rios | | Progresso de transcoding, read-only |\n| suggestions (owner) | processingErrors/\u2026/tagSuggestions/editorSuggestions (5) | list | | Recomenda\u00e7\u00f5es de otimiza\u00e7\u00e3o, read-only |\n| liveStreamingDetails | actualStartTime/actualEndTime/scheduledStartTime/scheduledEndTime/concurrentViewers/activeLiveChatId | v\u00e1rios | | Timing de transmiss\u00e3o, read-only |\n\n### 2.2 `channels` \u2014 parts: `snippet \u00b7 contentDetails \u00b7 statistics \u00b7 topicDetails \u00b7 status \u00b7 brandingSettings \u00b7 auditDetails \u00b7 contentOwnerDetails \u00b7 localizations` (fonte: [`channels.md`](./_apimap/channels.md))\n\n| Part | Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet | title/description/customUrl/publishedAt/thumbnails/defaultLanguage/localized/country | | 8 props read-only |\n| contentDetails | relatedPlaylists.likes/.favorites(deprec)/.uploads | | `uploads` = chave p/ listar todos os v\u00eddeos |\n| statistics | viewCount (inclui replay de Shorts desde 2025-03-31) | | Total de views |\n| statistics | subscriberCount (arred. 3 sig) / hiddenSubscriberCount / videoCount (s\u00f3 p\u00fablicos) / commentCount(deprec) | | N\u00fameros do canal |\n| topicDetails | topicIds(deprec)/topicCategories | | T\u00f3picos |\n| status | privacyStatus/isLinked/longUploadsStatus/madeForKids | | Status |\n| status | selfDeclaredMadeForKids | \u2705 | Declara\u00e7\u00e3o kids do dono |\n| brandingSettings | channel.title (\u226430) | \u26a0\ufe0f | **N\u00c3O edit\u00e1vel** \u2014 API exige valor = t\u00edtulo atual ou omitir (\u00a7 armadilha) |\n| brandingSettings | channel.description(\u22641000)/keywords(\u2264500)/trackingAnalyticsAccountId/unsubscribedTrailer/defaultLanguage/country | \u2705 | Configs de marca edit\u00e1veis |\n| brandingSettings | image.bannerExternalUrl | \u2705 | Fonte p/ gera\u00e7\u00e3o do banner (demais `image.*` e `watch.*` deprecated) |\n| auditDetails | overallGoodStanding/communityGuidelinesGoodStanding/copyrightStrikesGoodStanding/contentIdClaimsGoodStanding | | Sa\u00fade do canal \u2014 escopo `youtubepartner-channel-audit` |\n| contentOwnerDetails | contentOwner/timeLinked | | V\u00ednculo com content owner |\n| localizations | (key).title/.description | \u2705 | T\u00edtulo/descri\u00e7\u00e3o localizados |\n\n> **`channels.update` s\u00f3 aceita `part` = `brandingSettings` \\| `invideoPromotion` \\| `localizations`** (+ `status.selfDeclaredMadeForKids`), **um part por request**. Sem\u00e2ntica destrutiva-parcial: campo omitido de um part enviado \u00e9 APAGADO \u2192 sempre read-modify-write.\n\n### 2.3 `playlists` \u2014 parts: `snippet \u00b7 status \u00b7 contentDetails \u00b7 player \u00b7 localizations \u00b7 id` (fonte: [`playlists-playlistitems.md \u00a71`](./_apimap/playlists-playlistitems.md))\n\n| Propriedade | Tipo | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.publishedAt / channelId / channelTitle / thumbnails | v\u00e1rios | | Read-only |\n| snippet.title | string | \u2705 | T\u00edtulo (obrigat\u00f3rio no insert/update) |\n| snippet.description | string | \u2705 | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| snippet.defaultLanguage | string | \u2705 | Idioma |\n| status.privacyStatus | string | \u2705 | `private`/`public`/`unlisted` |\n| status.podcastStatus | string | \u2705 | Status de podcast |\n| contentDetails.itemCount | uint | | N\u00ba de itens |\n| player.embedHtml | string | | `<iframe>` da playlist |\n| localizations.(key).title/.description | object | \u2705 | Metadados localizados |\n\n### 2.4 `playlistItems` \u2014 parts: `snippet \u00b7 contentDetails \u00b7 status \u00b7 id` (fonte: [`playlists-playlistitems.md \u00a72`](./_apimap/playlists-playlistitems.md))\n\n| Propriedade | Tipo | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.publishedAt / channelId / channelTitle / videoOwnerChannelTitle / videoOwnerChannelId / thumbnails | v\u00e1rios | | Read-only |\n| snippet.title / description | string | \u2705 | Via recurso |\n| snippet.playlistId | string | (insert) | Playlist que cont\u00e9m o item |\n| snippet.position | uint | \u2705 | Posi\u00e7\u00e3o 0-based |\n| snippet.resourceId.kind / .videoId | string | (insert) | V\u00eddeo referenciado |\n| contentDetails.videoId / videoPublishedAt | v\u00e1rios | | Read-only |\n| contentDetails.note | string(\u2264280) | \u2705 | Nota do usu\u00e1rio |\n| contentDetails.startAt / endAt | string | \u2705(deprec) | Tempos de reprodu\u00e7\u00e3o (deprecados) |\n| status.privacyStatus | string | | Read-only |\n\n### 2.5 `search` (search result) \u2014 part: `snippet` (SOMENTE LEITURA \u2014 fonte: [`search.md`](./_apimap/search.md))\n\n| Propriedade | Tipo | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|---|:--:|---|\n| kind / etag | string | | `youtube#searchResult` |\n| id.kind | string | | `youtube#video`/`#channel`/`#playlist` |\n| id.videoId / id.channelId / id.playlistId | string | | ID conforme `id.kind` |\n| snippet.publishedAt / channelId / title / description / thumbnails / channelTitle / liveBroadcastContent | v\u00e1rios | | Todos read-only |\n\n> Filtro-chave do painel: `forMine=true` (+ `type=video`) = \"meus v\u00eddeos\". Orden\u00e1vel por `date`/`viewCount`/`rating`/`title`. Custo 1 unidade MAS teto de **100 chamadas/dia** no bucket Search Queries \u2192 paginar via `playlistItems.list` sobre a playlist de uploads \u00e9 mais barato.\n\n### 2.6 `commentThreads` + `comments` (fonte: [`comments.md`](./_apimap/comments.md))\n\n**`commentThreads`** \u2014 parts: `id \u00b7 replies \u00b7 snippet` (s\u00f3 `list`+`insert`):\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.channelId / videoId | | Alvo da thread |\n| snippet.topLevelComment | (insert) | `snippet.topLevelComment.snippet.textOriginal` escrito no insert |\n| snippet.canReply / totalReplyCount / isPublic | | Read-only |\n| replies.comments[] | | Amostra de respostas |\n\n**`comments`** \u2014 parts: `id \u00b7 snippet`:\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.authorDisplayName / authorProfileImageUrl / authorChannelUrl / authorChannelId.value | | Autor (read-only) |\n| snippet.channelId / textDisplay | | Read-only |\n| snippet.textOriginal | \u2705 | Texto (escrev\u00edvel em insert/update) |\n| snippet.parentId | \u2705(insert) | Coment\u00e1rio pai (resposta) |\n| snippet.canRate / viewerRating / likeCount | | Read-only |\n| snippet.moderationStatus | | `heldForReview`/`likelySpam`/`published`/`rejected` \u2014 muda via `setModerationStatus`, n\u00e3o no update |\n| snippet.publishedAt / updatedAt | | Timestamps |\n\n### 2.7 `captions` \u2014 parts: `id \u00b7 snippet` (fonte: [`captions.md`](./_apimap/captions.md))\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.videoId | \u2705(insert) | V\u00eddeo associado (obrigat\u00f3rio no insert, imut\u00e1vel depois) |\n| snippet.lastUpdated | | ISO 8601, read-only |\n| snippet.trackKind | \u2705(insert) | `ASR`/`forced`/`standard` |\n| snippet.language | \u2705(insert) | BCP-47 (obrigat\u00f3rio no insert) |\n| snippet.name | \u2705(insert) | \u2264150 chars (obrigat\u00f3rio no insert) |\n| snippet.audioTrackType | \u2705(insert) | `commentary`/`descriptive`/`primary`/`unknown` |\n| snippet.isCC / isLarge / isEasyReader | \u2705(insert) | Flags de acessibilidade |\n| snippet.isDraft | \u2705 | **\u00danico campo do snippet alter\u00e1vel no update** (+ novo arquivo) |\n| snippet.isAutoSynced / status / failureReason | | Read-only |\n\n### 2.8 `thumbnails` \u2014 sem GET pr\u00f3prio; s\u00f3 `set` (fonte: [`thumbnails-categories-sections-watermarks.md \u00a71`](./_apimap/thumbnails-categories-sections-watermarks.md))\n\nMiniaturas s\u00e3o lidas dentro de `snippet.thumbnails` de video/channel/playlist. `thumbnails.set` faz upload (\u22642 MB; `image/jpeg`,`image/png`,`application/octet-stream`) e substitui a miniatura custom do `videoId`. Resposta `youtube#thumbnailSetResponse`.\n\n### 2.9 `videoCategories` \u2014 part: `snippet` (SOMENTE LEITURA \u2014 fonte: [`\u2026watermarks.md \u00a72`](./_apimap/thumbnails-categories-sections-watermarks.md))\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| kind / etag / id | | `youtube#videoCategory` |\n| snippet.channelId / title / assignable | | Popula selects de categoria; `assignable` = pode associar v\u00eddeos |\n\n### 2.10 `channelSections` \u2014 parts: `id \u00b7 snippet \u00b7 contentDetails` (fonte: [`\u2026watermarks.md \u00a73`](./_apimap/thumbnails-categories-sections-watermarks.md))\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.type | \u2705 | `allPlaylists`/`completedEvents`/`liveEvents`/`multipleChannels`/`multiplePlaylists`/`popularUploads`/`recentUploads`/`singlePlaylist`/`subscriptions`/`upcomingEvents` |\n| snippet.channelId | | Read-only |\n| snippet.title | \u2705 | \u2264100 chars, s\u00f3 p/ `multiplePlaylists`/`multipleChannels` |\n| snippet.position | \u2705 | \u00cdndice 0-based na p\u00e1gina do canal |\n| contentDetails.playlists[] | \u2705 | Obrigat\u00f3rio p/ `singlePlaylist`/`multiplePlaylists` |\n| contentDetails.channels[] | \u2705 | Obrigat\u00f3rio p/ `multipleChannels` |\n\n> Limite: m\u00e1ximo **10 prateleiras** (shelves) por canal via `insert`.\n\n### 2.11 `subscriptions` \u2014 parts: `snippet \u00b7 contentDetails \u00b7 id \u00b7 subscriberSnippet` (S\u00d3 `list`/`insert`/`delete`; propriedades read-only \u2014 fonte: [`subscriptions-activities-i18n.md \u00a71`](./_apimap/subscriptions-activities-i18n.md))\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.publishedAt / title / description | | Read-only |\n| snippet.resourceId.channelId | (insert body) | Canal ao qual se inscreve \u2014 corpo m\u00ednimo do insert |\n| snippet.channelId / thumbnails | | Read-only |\n| contentDetails.totalItemCount / newItemCount / activityType | | Estat\u00edsticas (`uploads`/`all`) |\n| subscriberSnippet.title / description / channelId / thumbnails | | Dados do inscrito |\n\n### 2.12 `activities` \u2014 parts: `snippet \u00b7 contentDetails \u00b7 id` (SOMENTE LEITURA \u2014 `insert` obsoleto; fonte: [`subscriptions-activities-i18n.md \u00a72`](./_apimap/subscriptions-activities-i18n.md))\n\n| Propriedade | W | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o |\n|---|:--:|---|\n| snippet.publishedAt / channelId / title / description / thumbnails / channelTitle | | Read-only |\n| snippet.type | | `channelItem`/`comment`/`favorite`/`like`/`playlistItem`/`promotedItem`/`recommendation`/`social`/`subscription`/`upload` |\n| snippet.groupId | | Agrupa eventos do mesmo usu\u00e1rio/recurso |\n| contentDetails.upload.videoId / like.resourceId / favorite / comment / subscription.resourceId.channelId / playlistItem / recommendation / social / channelItem | | Payload conforme `snippet.type` (todos read-only) |\n\n> **Nota de obsolesc\u00eancia:** o recurso de channel bulletin foi removido \u2014 `activities.list` n\u00e3o recupera bulletins e `activities.insert` \u00e9 obsoleto. Sem m\u00e9todo de escrita utiliz\u00e1vel.\n\n### 2.13 (contexto) `i18nLanguages` / `i18nRegions` \u2014 p\u00fablicos, populam seletores `hl`/`gl`\n\n`i18nLanguages.list` \u2192 `snippet.hl`+`snippet.name` (BCP-47). `i18nRegions.list` \u2192 `snippet.gl`+`snippet.name` (ISO pa\u00eds). Sem escopo (API key basta). Fonte: [`subscriptions-activities-i18n.md \u00a73-4`](./_apimap/subscriptions-activities-i18n.md).\n\n---\n\n## \u00a73 Cat\u00e1logo YouTube Analytics API v2 (`reports.query`)\n\n> Fonte: [`analytics-metrics.md`](./_apimap/analytics-metrics.md) + [`analytics-dimensions-reports.md`](./_apimap/analytics-dimensions-reports.md). Base host `https://youtubeanalytics.googleapis.com/v2`. `reports.query` (GET `/reports`) par\u00e2metros: `ids` (`channel==MINE`), `startDate`, `endDate`, `metrics` (obrig.) + `dimensions`/`filters`/`sort`/`maxResults`/`currency`/`startIndex`/`includeHistoricalChannelData` (opc.).\n\n### \u00a73.1 M\u00e9tricas (52 \u00b7 monet\u00e1rias-de-valor = 6, + 2 contagens em relat\u00f3rios de escopo monet\u00e1rio)\n\n**Core (14 \u2014 sob Deprecation Policy):** `annotationClickThroughRate`, `annotationCloseRate`, `averageViewDuration`, `comments`, `dislikes`, `engagedViews`, `estimatedMinutesWatched`, `estimatedRevenue`\u2731, `likes`, `shares`, `subscribersGained`, `subscribersLost`, `viewerPercentage`, `views`.\n\n| Grupo | M\u00e9tricas | Escopo |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Views/Watch-time** | `views`, `engagedViews`, `redViews`, `viewerPercentage`, `estimatedMinutesWatched`, `estimatedRedMinutesWatched`, `averageViewDuration`, `averageViewPercentage` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Engajamento** | `comments`, `likes`, `dislikes`, `shares`, `subscribersGained`, `subscribersLost`, `videosAddedToPlaylists`, `videosRemovedFromPlaylists` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Anota\u00e7\u00f5es (7)** | `annotationImpressions`, `annotationClickableImpressions`, `annotationClicks`, `annotationClickThroughRate`, `annotationClosableImpressions`, `annotationCloses`, `annotationCloseRate` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Cards (6)** | `cardImpressions`, `cardClicks`, `cardClickRate`, `cardTeaserImpressions`, `cardTeaserClicks`, `cardTeaserClickRate` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Playlist (7)** | `playlistViews`, `playlistStarts`, `playlistSaves`, `averageTimeInPlaylist`, `playlistAverageViewDuration`, `playlistEstimatedMinutesWatched`, `viewsPerPlaylistStart` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Reten\u00e7\u00e3o (5)** | `audienceWatchRatio`, `relativeRetentionPerformance`, `startedWatching`, `stoppedWatching`, `totalSegmentImpressions` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Livestream (2)** | `averageConcurrentViewers`, `peakConcurrentViewers` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Membership (1)** | `membershipsCancellationSurveyResponses` | `yt-analytics.readonly` |\n| **Receita estimada \u2014 MONET\u00c1RIA** | `estimatedRevenue`\u2731, `estimatedAdRevenue`\u2731, `estimatedRedPartnerRevenue`\u2731 | `yt-analytics-monetary.readonly` |\n| **Ad performance \u2014 MONET\u00c1RIA** | `grossRevenue`\u2731, `cpm`\u2731, `playbackBasedCpm`\u2731, `adImpressions`, `monetizedPlaybacks` | `yt-analytics-monetary.readonly` |\n\n> \u2731 = valor monet\u00e1rio (aceita `currency`, default USD). **\u26a0\ufe0f Channel reports N\u00c3O suportam receita/ad performance** \u2014 a doc oficial de `channel_reports` diz \"*Estimated revenue and ad performance metrics are not currently supported for channel reports*\". As monet\u00e1rias s\u00f3 v\u00eam de **content-owner reports**. Um dashboard de canal (views\u2192reten\u00e7\u00e3o\u2192tr\u00e1fego\u2192demografia) \u00e9 100% coberto por `yt-analytics.readonly`.\n\n### \u00a73.2 Dimens\u00f5es (32 \u00b7 9 Core)\n\n**Core (9):** `ageGroup`, `channel`, `country`, `day`, `gender`, `month`, `sharingService`, `uploaderType`, `video`.\n\n| Categoria | Dimens\u00f5es |\n|---|---|\n| **Recursos** | `video`, `playlist`, `channel`, `group`(filter-only) |\n| **Tempo** | `day`, `month` |\n| **Geografia** | `country`, `province`(exige `country==US`), `dma`, `city`(desde 2022-01-01), `continent`(filter), `subContinent`(filter) |\n| **Playback location** | `insightPlaybackLocationType` (BROWSE/CHANNEL/EMBEDDED/EXTERNAL_APP/MOBILE/SEARCH/WATCH/YT_OTHER), `insightPlaybackLocationDetail` |\n| **Playback details** | `creatorContentType` (LIVE_STREAM/SHORTS/STORY/VIDEO_ON_DEMAND/UNSPECIFIED), `liveOrOnDemand`, `subscribedStatus`, `youtubeProduct` (CORE/GAMING/KIDS/MUSIC/UNKNOWN) |\n| **Traffic source** | `insightTrafficSourceType` (ADVERTISING/END_SCREEN/EXT_URL/NOTIFICATION/PLAYLIST/RELATED_VIDEO/SHORTS/SUBSCRIBER/YT_SEARCH/\u2026), `insightTrafficSourceDetail` |\n| **Device/OS** | `deviceType` (DESKTOP/GAME_CONSOLE/MOBILE/TABLET/TV/AUTOMOTIVE/WEARABLE/UNKNOWN_PLATFORM), `operatingSystem` (25 valores) |\n| **Demografia** | `ageGroup` (age13-17\u2026age65-), `gender` (female/male/user_specified) |\n| **Sharing** | `sharingService` (70+ plataformas) |\n| **Reten\u00e7\u00e3o** | `elapsedVideoTimeRatio` (0.01\u20131.0), `audienceType`(filter: ORGANIC/AD_INSTREAM/AD_INDISPLAY) |\n| **Live streaming** | `livestreamPosition` |\n| **Membership** | `membershipsCancellationSurveyReason` |\n| **Ad performance** | `adType` (auctionInstream/reservedBumperInstream/\u2026) |\n| **Content owner** | `claimedStatus`(filter: `claimed`), `uploaderType` (self/thirdParty) |\n\n### \u00a73.3 Tipos de relat\u00f3rio de canal (44 \u2014 32 v\u00eddeo + 12 playlist)\n\nTodos read-only via `yt-analytics.readonly`. Fonte: [`analytics-dimensions-reports.md \u00a75`](./_apimap/analytics-dimensions-reports.md).\n\n| Fam\u00edlia | Relat\u00f3rios | Dimens\u00e3o-chave \u00d7 m\u00e9tricas-chave |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Basic user activity** | #1, #2(US states) | (nenhuma dim) \u00d7 views, likes, comments, shares, subscribers, watch-time, annotation*, card* |\n| **Time-based** | #3, #4 | `day`/`month` \u00d7 conjunto completo + `uniques` |\n| **Geography** | #5, #6, #7(city \u2264250), #8(dma) | `country`/`province`/`city`/`dma` \u00d7 views, watch-time, avgViewDuration |\n| **Playback details** | #9\u2013#16 | `subscribedStatus`/`liveOrOnDemand`/`youtubeProduct` \u00d7 redViews, watch-time, avgViewPercentage |\n| **Playback location** | #17, #18(detail \u226425) | `insightPlaybackLocationType/Detail` \u00d7 engagedViews, views, minutes |\n| **Traffic source** | #19, #20(detail \u226425) | `insightTrafficSourceType/Detail` \u00d7 engagedViews, views, minutes |\n| **Device/OS** | #21, #22, #23 | `deviceType`+`operatingSystem` \u00d7 engagedViews, views, minutes |\n| **Demographics** | #24 | `ageGroup`+`gender` \u00d7 `viewerPercentage` |\n| **Engagement/sharing** | #25 | `sharingService` \u00d7 `shares` |\n| **Audience retention** | #26 | `elapsedVideoTimeRatio` \u00d7 audienceWatchRatio, relativeRetentionPerformance, started/stoppedWatching |\n| **Concurrent viewers** | #27 | `livestreamPosition` \u00d7 average/peakConcurrentViewers |\n| **Top videos** | #28\u2013#32 (\u2264200) | `video` \u00d7 conjunto completo (sort obrigat\u00f3rio) |\n| **Playlist** | #33\u2013#44 | `day`/`country`/`deviceType`/`ageGroup` \u00d7 playlistViews, playlistStarts, viewsPerPlaylistStart, averageTimeInPlaylist |\n\n> **Quota Analytics:** as docs oficiais N\u00c3O declaram custo-por-unidade para a Analytics v2 \u2014 \u00e9 limite de requisi\u00e7\u00f5es/dia no Cloud Console, n\u00e3o custo-por-m\u00e9todo `(n\u00e3o verificado)`.\n\n---\n\n## \u00a74 Registro de A\u00c7\u00d5ES (write / destrutivo) \u2014 escopo \u00b7 quota \u00b7 TIER de risco\n\n> **TIER:** `BAIXO` = editar metadados/thumbnail (revers\u00edvel via re-edit) \u00b7 `M\u00c9DIO` = playlist/coment\u00e1rio/subscription/se\u00e7\u00e3o/watermark/grupo (revers\u00edvel) \u00b7 `ALTO` = delete/irrevers\u00edvel. Fonte de custos: [`scopes-quota.md \u00a72`](./_apimap/scopes-quota.md) + method-pages.\n\n| A\u00e7\u00e3o | M\u00e9todo | Verbo | Escopo exigido | Quota | TIER |\n|---|---|---|---|---|:--:|\n| Editar metadados de v\u00eddeo | `videos.update` | PUT | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | BAIXO |\n| Trocar thumbnail | `thumbnails.set` | POST | `youtube.upload`/`youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | BAIXO |\n| Editar branding do canal | `channels.update` | PUT | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | BAIXO |\n| Avaliar v\u00eddeo (like/dislike/none) | `videos.rate` | POST | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | BAIXO |\n| Publicar v\u00eddeo (upload) | `videos.insert` | POST | `youtube.upload`/`youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 100 | BAIXO |\n| Editar playlist | `playlists.update` | PUT | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Criar playlist | `playlists.insert` | POST | idem | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Adicionar v\u00eddeo a playlist | `playlistItems.insert` | POST | idem | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Reordenar/editar item de playlist | `playlistItems.update` | PUT | idem | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Criar se\u00e7\u00e3o do canal | `channelSections.insert` | POST | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Editar se\u00e7\u00e3o do canal | `channelSections.update` | PUT | idem | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Inscrever-se em canal | `subscriptions.insert` | POST | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Postar coment\u00e1rio de topo | `commentThreads.insert` | POST | `youtube.force-ssl` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Responder a coment\u00e1rio | `comments.insert` | POST | `youtube.force-ssl` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Editar pr\u00f3prio coment\u00e1rio | `comments.update` | PUT | `youtube.force-ssl` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Moderar coment\u00e1rio (aprovar/rejeitar/hold) | `comments.setModerationStatus` | POST | `youtube.force-ssl` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Definir watermark do canal | `watermarks.set` | POST | `youtube.upload`/`youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Adicionar caption | `captions.insert` | POST | `youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 400 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Atualizar caption (isDraft/arquivo) | `captions.update` | PUT | `youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 450 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Denunciar v\u00eddeo (abuse) | `videos.reportAbuse` | POST | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Criar grupo Analytics | `groups.insert` | POST | `youtube`/`youtubepartner` | n/d | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Editar grupo Analytics (t\u00edtulo) | `groups.update` | PUT | `youtube`/`youtubepartner` | n/d | M\u00c9DIO |\n| Add item a grupo Analytics | `groupItems.insert` | POST | `youtube`/`youtubepartner` | n/d | M\u00c9DIO |\n| **Deletar v\u00eddeo** | `videos.delete` | DELETE | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Deletar playlist** | `playlists.delete` | DELETE | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Remover item de playlist** | `playlistItems.delete` | DELETE | idem | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Deletar coment\u00e1rio** | `comments.delete` | DELETE | `youtube.force-ssl` | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Deletar caption** | `captions.delete` | DELETE | `youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Deletar se\u00e7\u00e3o do canal** | `channelSections.delete` | DELETE | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Cancelar inscri\u00e7\u00e3o** | `subscriptions.delete` | DELETE | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | **ALTO** |\n| **Remover watermark** | `watermarks.unset` | POST | `youtube`/`youtube.force-ssl`/`youtubepartner` | 50 | **ALTO** (revers\u00edvel via `set`, mas remove branding) |\n| **Deletar grupo Analytics** | `groups.delete` | DELETE | `youtube`/`youtubepartner` | n/d | **ALTO** |\n| **Remover item de grupo Analytics** | `groupItems.delete` | DELETE | `youtube`/`youtubepartner` | n/d | **ALTO** |\n\n**Totais de superf\u00edcie:** 21 recursos Data API v3 \u00b7 49 m\u00e9todos \u2192 **19 read \u00b7 23 write \u00b7 7 destrutivos** (Data API) + Analytics v2 (3 read \u00b7 3 write \u00b7 2 destrutivos). Todo `delete` de sucesso = HTTP 204 No Content, **irrevers\u00edvel** (exceto watermark) \u2192 gate de confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla obrigat\u00f3rio (\u00a77 Fatia 3).\n\n---\n\n## \u00a75 Custos de quota\n\n> **Default: 10.000 unidades/dia** por projeto (reset meia-noite Pacific Time). Aloca\u00e7\u00e3o = 100 `search.list` + 100 `videos.insert` + 10.000 combinadas p/ demais endpoints. Toda request custa \u22651, mesmo inv\u00e1lida. Pagina\u00e7\u00e3o cobra por p\u00e1gina. Fonte: [`scopes-quota.md \u00a72`](./_apimap/scopes-quota.md) (`youtube/v3/determine_quota_cost`).\n\n| Opera\u00e7\u00e3o | Custo (un) | Classe |\n|---|---:|---|\n| `*.list` de leitura (activities, channels, channelSections, comments, commentThreads, playlistItems, playlists, subscriptions, videos, videoCategories, i18n*) | **1** | R |\n| `videos.getRating` | 1 | R |\n| `search.list` | **100** (+ teto 100 chamadas/dia no bucket Search Queries) | R |\n| `captions.list` | 50 | R |\n| `captions.download` | 200 | R |\n| Writes comuns (insert/update/delete de playlists, playlistItems, comments, commentThreads, subscriptions, channels, channelSections, watermarks; videos.update/rate/reportAbuse; thumbnails.set) | **50** | W/D |\n| `videos.insert` (upload) | **100** (reduzido de ~1600 em 2025-12-04) | W |\n| `captions.insert` | 400 | W |\n| `captions.update` | 450 | W |\n| Analytics v2 (`reports.query`, groups*, groupItems*) | limite req/dia (sem custo-por-unidade documentado) `(n\u00e3o verificado)` | R/W/D |\n\n### \u00a75.1 Or\u00e7amento de LEITURA do painel (Fatia 1)\n\nCarregar a grade + dashboard de um canal, por refresh:\n- **Listar \"meus v\u00eddeos\":** `playlistItems.list` sobre a playlist de uploads (`contentDetails.relatedPlaylists.uploads`) = **1 un/p\u00e1gina** (50 v\u00eddeos/p\u00e1gina). Preferir isto a `search.list?forMine` (100 un + teto 100/dia).\n- **Hidratar metadados+stats:** `videos.list?part=snippet,contentDetails,status,statistics&id=\u2026` = **1 un** por lote de at\u00e9 50 IDs.\n- **Canal + n\u00fameros agregados:** `channels.list?part=snippet,statistics,contentDetails&mine=true` = **1 un**.\n- **Categorias p/ selects:** `videoCategories.list` = **1 un** (cache\u00e1vel por regi\u00e3o).\n- **M\u00e9tricas por v\u00eddeo:** `reports.query` (Analytics v2) = fora do custo-por-unidade da Data API (limite req/dia).\n\n\u21d2 Um refresh completo de painel de canal com \u226450 v\u00eddeos custa **~4 unidades** da Data API + N chamadas Analytics. Com 10.000/dia, isso comporta **~2.500 refreshes/dia** \u2014 leitura \u00e9 praticamente gratuita; o gargalo real \u00e9 `search.list` (evit\u00e1vel) e uploads/captions (writes caros).\n\n---\n\n## \u00a76 Declara\u00e7\u00e3o de Conformidade de Padr\u00f5es (Pattern Conformance)\n\n> Contra os **21 padr\u00f5es ag\u00eanticos** de [`docs/architecture/agentic-vision.md`](../../architecture/agentic-vision.md). Declara\u00e7\u00e3o, n\u00e3o sign-off (CLAUDE.md \u00a71 Step 3.5). Escopo: o painel `youtube-studio` (console de leitura+m\u00e9tricas+a\u00e7\u00f5es).\n\n| # | Pattern | Implemented? | How / Why-deferred |\n|---|---|:--:|---|\n| 1 | Prompt Chaining | n-a | Painel \u00e9 CRUD data-driven sobre a API do Google; n\u00e3o h\u00e1 cadeia de prompts LLM neste m\u00f3dulo (a gera\u00e7\u00e3o de conte\u00fado vive em `orchestrate-content`, upstream). |\n| 2 | Routing | yes | Roteamento de resolu\u00e7\u00e3o de credencial per-user (`user_api_keys`/OAuth token do canal) antes de env global \u2014 API Tenancy Model. |\n| 3 | Parallelization | yes | Fan-out de leitura: `videos.list` em lotes de 50 IDs + `reports.query` por v\u00eddeo em paralelo no hook de dashboard. |\n| 4 | Reflection | deferred | Sem Generator-Critic aqui (OTD-AG-003). Vision-QA aplica-se s\u00f3 a criativos gerados, n\u00e3o \u00e0 grade de dados YouTube. |\n| 5 | Tool Use | yes | A pr\u00f3pria API YouTube (Data v3 + Analytics v2) \u00e9 a ferramenta tipada; edge functions `youtube-*` encapsulam cada m\u00e9todo com schema. |\n| 6 | Planning | yes | Plano de Fatias (\u00a77) com gate de materialidade por fatia; BoK suite governa o SSD do m\u00f3dulo. |\n| 7 | Multi-Agent | deferred | Painel single-surface. Delega\u00e7\u00e3o a agentes Pantheon (ex.: modera\u00e7\u00e3o de coment\u00e1rios pelo artisan) diferida at\u00e9 Fatia 2+. |\n| 8 | Memory Management | yes | N\u00f3s de observa\u00e7\u00e3o na Knowledge Mesh (`mcorch_nodes`) por sync bem-sucedido; cache de metadados de canal por `project_id`. |\n| 9 | Learning & Adaptation | deferred | Sem loop de feedback fechado sobre performance de v\u00eddeo\u2192estrat\u00e9gia (OTD-AG-002). S\u00f3 leitura descritiva na Fatia 1. |\n| 10 | Model Context Protocol | n-a | O painel consome a API REST do Google diretamente; n\u00e3o \u00e9 um servidor MCP (Vision MCP \u00e9 m\u00f3dulo separado). |\n| 11 | Goal Setting & Monitoring | yes | Telemetria em `infra_health_logs` (`service='youtube-studio'`) em cada path de sync (success/degraded/error). |\n| 12 | Exception Handling & Recovery | yes | Retry idempotente em 5xx/quota-exceeded (HTTP 403 `quotaExceeded`), refresh-token reauth gate, backoff no bucket Search. |\n| 13 | Human-in-the-Loop | yes | ORO + confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla obrigat\u00f3ria em toda a\u00e7\u00e3o TIER ALTO (delete irrevers\u00edvel \u2014 \u00a77 Fatia 3); GO Sovereign p/ escopos restricted. |\n| 14 | Knowledge Retrieval (RAG) | deferred | Sem RAG sobre o corpus YouTube na Fatia 1; leitura direta da API. Enriquecimento sem\u00e2ntico via mesh diferido. |\n| 15 | Inter-Agent Comm. (A2A) | n-a | Sem troca inter-agente neste m\u00f3dulo; painel \u00e9 superf\u00edcie de usu\u00e1rio. |\n| 16 | Resource-Aware Optimization | yes | Or\u00e7amento de quota expl\u00edcito (\u00a75.1): preferir `playlistItems.list` (1 un) a `search.list` (100 un + teto 100/dia); d\u00e9bito mcoCoins por a\u00e7\u00e3o. |\n| 17 | Reasoning Techniques | n-a | CRUD determin\u00edstico; sem racioc\u00ednio LLM na leitura/escrita da API. |\n| 18 | Guardrails / Safety | yes | Fail-closed em escopo ausente (o gap `youtube.upload`-only vira 402/erro estruturado, n\u00e3o sil\u00eancio); RLS default-deny nos tokens; ES256 JWT; Cyber-Sentinel; confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla destrutiva. |\n| 19 | Evaluation & Monitoring | yes | Smoke re-execut\u00e1vel por m\u00e9todo antes de mudar integra\u00e7\u00e3o (padr\u00e3o `smoke-higgsfield-schema`); auditoria de quota consumida. |\n| 20 | Prioritization | yes | TIER de risco (\u00a74) prioriza ordem de implementa\u00e7\u00e3o (BAIXO\u2192M\u00c9DIO\u2192ALTO) e gate de confirma\u00e7\u00e3o; Fatias priorizam leitura antes de escrita. |\n| 21 | Exploration & Discovery | deferred | `search.list` habilita descoberta, mas sem torneio/ranking de explora\u00e7\u00e3o (OTD-AG-008); trends de canal via autopilot diferido. |\n\n**Placar do m\u00f3dulo:** yes 12 \u00b7 deferred 6 \u00b7 n-a 5. Os `deferred` herdam OTDs existentes da doutrina (AG-002/003/008) ou s\u00e3o naturais de um painel de leitura-primeiro; nenhum \u00e9 gap arquitetural novo.\n\n---\n\n## \u00a77 Plano de Fatias (gate de materialidade cada)\n\n### Fatia 1 \u2014 Tabela CRUD read-only de v\u00eddeos + TODAS as m\u00e9tricas \u00b7 **\"o primeiro de tudo\"**\n\n**Escopos:** `youtube.readonly` + `yt-analytics.readonly` (+ `-monetary.readonly` se surface de receita) via **re-consent OAuth incremental** (o `youtube.upload`-only atual N\u00c3O serve \u2014 \u00a71).\n**Superf\u00edcie:** grade de v\u00eddeos (`playlistItems.list` uploads \u2192 `videos.list` hidrata snippet/status/statistics) + dashboard por v\u00eddeo (`reports.query`: views, watch-time, reten\u00e7\u00e3o, tr\u00e1fego, device, demografia, geografia). Colunas = \u00a72.1; m\u00e9tricas = \u00a73.1; relat\u00f3rios = \u00a73.3.\n**Gate de materialidade:** `curl` real de `videos.list` retornando \u22651 v\u00eddeo do canal conectado (HTTP 200 + `items[].id`) **E** `reports.query` retornando \u22651 linha de m\u00e9trica (HTTP 200) **E** screenshot 1920\u00d71080 da grade renderizada com dados reais (Vision QA). Sem prova dos 3 \u2192 n\u00e3o declarar Fatia 1 pronta (Lei 1).\n**Recovery:** se re-consent n\u00e3o concede `youtube.readonly` (usu\u00e1rio desmarca) \u2192 detectar via `scope` do token, mostrar CTA de reconex\u00e3o, fail-closed (n\u00e3o tentar `search`/`videos.list` que retornariam 403).\n\n### Fatia 2 \u2014 A\u00e7\u00f5es de escrita segura (metadados / thumbnail / playlist) \u00b7 TIER BAIXO+M\u00c9DIO\n\n**Escopos:** `youtube` (metadados de v\u00eddeo/playlist/se\u00e7\u00e3o) + `youtube.upload` (thumbnail) + `youtube.force-ssl` (coment\u00e1rio/modera\u00e7\u00e3o).\n**Superf\u00edcie:** editar t\u00edtulo/descri\u00e7\u00e3o/tags/privacy (`videos.update`, read-modify-write da part inteira \u2014 armadilha PUT \u00a72.1); trocar thumbnail (`thumbnails.set` \u22642 MB); CRUD de playlist e playlistItems; responder/moderar coment\u00e1rios. Toda a\u00e7\u00e3o debita mcoCoins e emite `infra_health_logs`.\n**Gate de materialidade:** um `videos.update` real alterando o t\u00edtulo de um v\u00eddeo de teste \u2192 GET subsequente confirma o novo valor (HTTP 200 + t\u00edtulo mudado); um `thumbnails.set` retornando `youtube#thumbnailSetResponse`. Prova por leitura-de-volta, n\u00e3o por status 2xx cego.\n**Recovery:** PUT parcial que apagou campo (sem\u00e2ntica destrutiva-parcial) \u2192 re-aplicar snapshot pr\u00e9-edi\u00e7\u00e3o guardado antes do write; quota 403 \u2192 fila com backoff.\n\n### Fatia 3 \u2014 A\u00e7\u00f5es destrutivas com confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla + dry-run \u00b7 TIER ALTO\n\n**Escopos:** `youtube.force-ssl` (delete de v\u00eddeo/coment\u00e1rio/caption) + `youtube` (delete de playlist/se\u00e7\u00e3o/subscription).\n**Superf\u00edcie:** `videos.delete`, `playlists.delete`, `playlistItems.delete`, `comments.delete`, `captions.delete`, `channelSections.delete`, `subscriptions.delete`, `watermarks.unset`. **Cada a\u00e7\u00e3o: (1) dry-run** que mostra exatamente o que ser\u00e1 removido (t\u00edtulo, ID, contagem de views/itens afetados via leitura pr\u00e9via) + **(2) confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla** (digitar o ID ou \"EXCLUIR\") antes do DELETE. Enquadra-se na doutrina smart-delete/transpar\u00eancia do MCORCH.\n**Gate de materialidade:** um `videos.delete` de v\u00eddeo de teste retornando HTTP 204 **E** `videos.list?id=` subsequente retornando `items: []` (some do canal). Prova por aus\u00eancia-confirmada, n\u00e3o por 204 cego.\n**Recovery:** DELETE irrevers\u00edvel \u2192 **n\u00e3o h\u00e1 undo na API** (por isso a confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla \u00e9 o gate real); registrar em `infra_health_logs` + n\u00f3 de observa\u00e7\u00e3o na mesh todo delete executado, com o payload lido no dry-run, para trilha de auditoria (o dado n\u00e3o volta, mas o registro do que existia sim).\n\n---\n\n## \u00a78 Fontes (rodap\u00e9 de materialidade \u2014 Lei 1)\n\nTodos os 11 mapas em [`_apimap/`](./_apimap/) citam as URLs oficiais Google efetivamente abertas via WebFetch em 2026-07-12. Verificado nas docs: strings de escopo + o-que-concede, propriedades+writable por recurso, custos de quota da Data API v3, 52 m\u00e9tricas + 32 dimens\u00f5es + 44 relat\u00f3rios de canal da Analytics v2. **`(n\u00e3o verificado)`** persistente: classifica\u00e7\u00e3o nominal sensitive/restricted por escopo e gatilho CASA \u2014 a p\u00e1gina `identity/protocols/oauth2/scopes` truncou antes da se\u00e7\u00e3o YouTube em toda captura; confirmar em janela que n\u00e3o trunque antes de qualquer claim de compliance. Custo-por-unidade da Analytics v2 = limite req/dia, n\u00e3o documentado por m\u00e9todo.\n\n*Gerado por MCORCH API-surface synthesis agent \u00b7 2026-07-12 \u00b7 SSOT do painel youtube-studio.*\n"
}

- **Escopos OAuth:** 9 (readonly, youtube, upload, force-ssl, channel-memberships, youtubepartner, partner-channel-audit, yt-analytics.readonly, yt-analytics-monetary.readonly)
- **Recursos Data API v3:** 13 (videos, channels, playlists, playlistItems, search, commentThreads, comments, captions, thumbnails, videoCategories, channelSections, subscriptions, activities)
- **Propriedades totais** (as colunas da tabela CRUD): 175
- **Métricas Analytics v2:** 52 (6 monetárias-de-valor)
- **Dimensões:** 32 (9 Core)
- **Relatórios de canal:** 44 (documentados no §3.3)
- **Ações write/destrutivas:** 33 → **BAIXO 5 · MÉDIO 18 · ALTO 10**

**Achado decisivo destacado em caixa (§1):** a conexão de hoje só tem `youtube.upload` — que publica mas **não lê vídeos nem métricas**. A Fatia 1 está bloqueada até um re-consent OAuth incremental adicionar `youtube.readonly` + `yt-analytics.readonly`. O sinal do gap é material: o token OAuth carrega o campo `scope` concedido (o próprio fluxo `installed-apps` avisa que "usuários podem não conceder todos").

**Estrutura do `11-api-surface-map.md`** (460 linhas): §1 escopos+caixa-do-gap+fluxo OAuth · §2 os 13 recursos com propriedades/writable · §3 catálogo Analytics (52 métricas por grupo, 32 dimensões, 44 relatórios) · §4 registro de 33 ações por escopo/quota/TIER · §5 quota (10.000/dia + orçamento de leitura: refresh completo ≈ 4 unidades) · §6 Pattern Conformance dos 21 padrões (yes 12 · deferred 6 · n-a 5) · §7 plano de 3 Fatias com gate de materialidade cada (leitura+métricas → escrita segura → destrutivo com dry-run + confirmação dupla).

**Materialidade (Lei 1):** tudo citado nas URLs oficiais dos mapas; sensibilidade/CASA por escopo carrega `(não verificado)` + flag `unverified:true` no JSON porque a página `oauth2/scopes` truncou antes da seção YouTube em toda captura.

**Caminhos gravados:**
- `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/docs/bok/youtube-studio/11-api-surface-map.md` — **460 linhas**
- `/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/docs/bok/youtube-studio/youtube-api-registry.json` — **460 linhas** (JSON válido; nota: `quotaCost:null` nas 6 ações de grupos Analytics, cujo custo-por-unidade não é documentado pelo Google — é limite req/dia).
